  const IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags-ts';
  const IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
  function returnIXPDataFromCacheIfValid() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
      return null;
    }

    try {
      const rawFlags = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
      if (!rawFlags || rawFlags === '{}') {
        return null;
      }

      const parsedFlags = JSON.parse(rawFlags);
      if (!parsedFlags || typeof parsedFlags !== 'object') {
        return null;
      }

      const rawTimestamp = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
      const now = Date.now();

      if (!rawTimestamp) {
        return null;
      }

      const cachedAt = Number(rawTimestamp);
      if (Number.isFinite(cachedAt) && now - cachedAt < IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
        return parsedFlags;
      }

      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Failed to read IXP cache from sessionStorage:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }
   // adding this dummy function here to avoid page break of buy/internet page. that page is calling this function
  function unmountGlobalNavScripts() {
    console.log('unmountGlobalNavScripts called latest..');
    const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
    if (isNativeApp) {
      const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
      gnavHeaderId?.remove();
    }
  }
  function getMeganavCookie(lookup) {
    let nameEQ = 'accessDomain' + '=';
    const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    if (typeof lookup !== 'undefined') {
      nameEQ = lookup + '=';
    }
    for (let c of ca) {
      while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
        c = c.substring(1, c.length);
      }
      if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
        return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
      }
    }
  }
  const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
  if (isNativeApp) {
    const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
    gnavHeaderId?.remove();
